Anime 3
⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢀⣀⣀⡰⡏⠉⠓⠦⣄⠀⠀⠀⣀⣀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀

⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢸⣞⠁⢀⡠⢀⡼⠓⠊⠉⠀⠉⠉⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠉⠈⠙⠦⣄⠀⠀⠒⠶⣞⡴⠃⠀⠀⢰⡇⠀⠀
⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠸⡄⡽⠛⡴⠋⡀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⡞⡆⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⠳⣄⠀⠀⠀⠉⠳⢄⡀⣸⠀⠀⠀

⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⡎⠀⡠⢰⠃⣠⠞⠉⠉⠉⡙⠀⠀⠀⠀⡀⢀⠀⠆⠀⡇⢸⠀⢸⠀⠘⢄⠙⢶⣄⠀⠘⣆⠀⠀⠐⢆⠀⢹⡀
⠠⣄⣀⠲⢤⣧⣞⣀⡾⠜⠁⠀⡄⣤⡀⣇⡆⡀⠀⣄⣷⣧⣦⠸⣴⢰⠈⡄⠸⠀⠐⣌⠻⢄⣉⣲⡤⢼⡄⠀⠀⠈⢳⡦⠇
⠀⠈⠹⢿⣽⣛⣛⠛⠋⠁⢀⣸⡜⠈⢿⣿⣷⣇⠀⢿⡇⠈⠻⢷⣌⢧⠂⠁⡄⢀⠀⠈⠢⡀⢌⡉⠳⠤⣇⣀⣀⣀⣀⣳⡄
⠀⢠⣀⣠⡭⠶⠶⠾⡑⣠⣿⠿⠷⠀⠀⠙⢻⣿⣄⣼⡓⠀⠀⠀⡨⡿⠳⡄⢱⢸⡄⠀⠀⢈⢀⠘⠭⢗⣶⣿⡿⠛⠁⠀⣧
⠀⠀⠀⠉⠑⣖⣲⣋⡴⠋⢡⠒⢄⡀⠀⠀⠀⠈⠙⠿⣧⠀⠀⠀⠉⣠⡄⠘⠦⣿⣏⠢⡀⢄⠑⡭⣻⣖⣲⠛⠉⠁⠀⠀⢸

Anime 4

⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠄⣫⠀⠀⡰⢁⢈⠈⠁⠀⠀⠠⣼⣄⠂⠀⠡⠞⣃⣩⠛⡀⠀⠆⠀⠀⠠⠁⢀⠀⠀⠀⠈⢦⡀⠀⠀

Anime 5
⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢀⡔⠒⢶⠉⣍⣙⠃⠲⠦⢄⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀

⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⣼⠆⢀⢠⡔⠉⣎⢜⣴⣀⡇⠈⠳⣤⣀⢰⡀⢀⢀⠹⡸⣙⣓⣻⠁⢀⡾⠁⠈⣦⣿⠎⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀
⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⡰⡍⠀⢸⡼⣄⣀⣸⣆⢨⢻⣧⡤⠶⠾⠿⢿⡇⠈⠂⡆⠳⣯⣉⡇⠀⢈⠀⢀⢠⣻⣅⣀⣀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀
⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠁⡇⣄⢸⡀⠀⠀⠀⠘⠦⠑⠵⠕⠀⣀⣠⣈⡹⣄⢢⢣⢠⠘⡍⡛⢿⡣⡀⢀⡾⣷⡹⣍⠁⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀

⠠⡦⠤⡲⣛⡵⠂⡜⢸⢸⠀⠀⠀⠁⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠠⢮⡞⠀⡰⣴⣿⢛⡩⠎⠀⢧⡳⣵⠤⠴⠃⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀

⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢀⡶⢡⣾⢟⠎⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠘⡟⡅⠀⢀⠎⠀⡘⢀⠎⣼⠞⠉⠙⠢⡀⠀⠘⡏⠀⠀⠉⠺⣧⢣⠀⠀

Anime 6
⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⣀⣠⠴⠖⠚⠋⠉⡙⠛⢙⣲⣶⣤⣤⣤⣄⡀⠀⠠⡀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀

Anime 12

Anime 26

Anime dot art turns anime-style character drawings into copy-paste text pictures made of Braille dots. Each piece is a full figure — big expressive eyes, flowing hair, a detailed outfit — rendered in tiny dots that stay sharp wherever you paste. Made for the weeb, otaku, and anime-profile crowd who want a whole character in plain text.

When to use Anime dot art

  • Repping your weeb / otaku side in a Discord bio or anime server
  • Decorating a profile, username, or caption with an anime figure
  • Dropping a character into a YouTube or anime-community comment

💡 Platform tip: Anime dot art is a comment-section and Discord classic — anime communities pass these around constantly, especially in YouTube comments. Being plain Braille text, a piece pastes into any bio, comment, or status with no upload — Copy, paste, done.

FAQ

Frequently Asked Questions

What is anime dot art?
Anime dot art is a multi-line text picture of an anime-style character built from Braille dot characters (Unicode ⠀–⣿). Each character holds up to 8 dots, enough to shape the big eyes, hair and outfit. You copy it as text and drop it under a video or into a bio.
How is it different from an anime emoji or pfp?
An emoji or profile picture is an uploaded image. Anime dot art is plain text — it pastes straight into a comment or bio with no upload, can’t be blocked as an attachment, and grows or shrinks with your font.
Where does anime dot art look best?
YouTube comments and Discord are where it belongs — both hold the line spacing so the figure keeps its form. Dark themes make it pop, and dropping it in a code block locks every line into place.
Will it work on my phone?
Every modern phone and computer already carries these characters, so there’s nothing to download. A tall figure can break across lines in a slim box — landscape mode or a wider field snaps it back together.
Why is anime dot art so popular in comments?
It started as a comment-section flex — dropping a whole anime character built from text under a video or post is more eye-catching than an emoji, and it can’t be stripped out the way an image link can. That’s why anime communities keep passing them around.
